# Crossword fill list: words the grid filler may use, grouped by SCOWL size level (10 = most
# common, 60 = least). Built from the SCOWL word lists (wordlist-english 1.2.1, english and
# american, levels 10-60), keeping only words that are also in ENABLE and not on the site's
# blocklist, 3 to 11 letters. SCOWL is (c) Kevin Atkinson and others; see licenses/SCOWL-Copyright.txt.
# The filler prefers common words and words that already have clues in crossword-clues.txt.
